Title: История Выговской старообрядческой пустыни (History of the Vyg Old Believer Hermitage), published from the manuscript of Ivan Filippov, with his orthography preserved, eleven portraits of notable Old Believers and two views of the Vyg men's and women's communities
Creator: Ivan Filippov (1661-1744), abbot (nastoiatel') of Vyg
Publisher: St Petersburg: D. E. Kozhanchikov (the imprint is not legible in the scan; the host gives 1862)
Publication Date: 1862
Rights: Public Domain (published 1862, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: rus
Source: Internet Archive item istorijavygovskojstaroobrjadcheskojpusty61 (https://archive.org/details/istorijavygovskojstaroobrjadcheskojpusty61), the item's own istorijavygovskojstaroobrjadcheskojpusty61_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1862. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: a4a8a8bb9ae5ac39faaed3f01dc29af2b73f31cd2693be3dc2470e85b7e1b448.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Scan contributed to the Internet Archive without a named library; the title page is partly garbled, the body readable in the old orthography.
Content note: The Vyg community's own history from its foundation in 1694 to the 1740s, written by its abbot Ivan Filippov: the Denisovs, the community's rule and economy, its dealings with Peter's state and the Synod; the communities' own layer the census names, from inside.

въ Палеостровоюи монастырь, на Онежское озеро. Мноме 
отправлепы въ заточеше въ Соловецюй монастырь. „Личное 
присутстые въ Соловецкомъ монастыр$ и въ Олонецкихь
